## Overview

- Files: **920**

- README: Yappy **Yappy** is an infinite canvas drawing and diagramming application built with SolidJS. Create hand-drawn style diagrams, architecture sketches, mindmaps, wireframes, presentations, and whiteboard illustrations with 100+ shape types and a full-featured toolset.
